```text
6. haga el reporte útil para DJ Mauch: por workstream/proyecto y por topic/canal, no por “session” abstracta.

## Constraints & Preferences
- DJ Mauch es el usuario principal.

- Mantener el principio de no romper ejecución normal si falla la contabilidad.
```

```text
{"success": true, "name": "hermes-agent", "description": "Configure, extend, or contribute to Hermes Agent.", "tags": ["hermes", "setup", "configuration", "multi-agent", "spawning", "cli", "gateway", "development"], "related_skills": ["claude-code", "codex", "opencode"], "content": "---\nname: hermes-agent\ndescription: \"Configure, extend, or contribute to Hermes Agent.\"\nversion: 2.1.0\nauthor: Hermes Agent + Teknium\nlicense: MIT\nplatforms: [linux, macos, windows]\nmetadata:\n  hermes:\n    tags: [hermes, setup, configuration, multi-agent, spawning, cli, gateway, development]\n    homepage: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent\n    related_skills: [claude-code, codex, opencode]\n---\n\n# Hermes Agent\n\nHermes Agent is an open-source AI agent framework by Nous Research that runs in your terminal, messaging platforms, and IDEs. It belongs to the same category as Claude Code (Anthropic), Codex (OpenAI), and OpenClaw — autonomous coding and task-execution agents that use tool calling to interact with your system. Hermes works with any LLM provider (OpenRouter, Anthropic, OpenAI, DeepSeek, local models, and 15+ others) and runs on Linux, macOS, and WSL.\n\nWhat makes Hermes different:\n\n- **Self-improving through skills** — Hermes learns from experience by saving reusable procedures as skills. When it solves a complex problem, discovers a workflow, or gets corrected, it can persist that knowledge as a skill document that loads into future sessions. Skills accumulate over time, making the agent better at your specific tasks and environment.\n- **Persistent memory across sessions** — remembers who you are, your preferences, environment details, and lessons learned. Pluggable memory backends (built-in, Honcho, Mem0, and more) let you choose how memory works.\n- **Multi-platform gateway** — the same agent runs on Telegram, Discord, Slack, WhatsApp, Signal, Matrix, Email, and 10+ other platforms with full tool access, not just chat.\n- **Provider-agnostic** — swap models and providers mid-workflow without changing anything else. Credential pools rotate across multiple API keys automatically.\n- **Profiles** — run multiple independent Hermes instances with isolated configs, sessions, skills, and memory.\n- **Extensible** — plugins, MCP servers, custom tools, webhook triggers, cron scheduling, and the full Python ecosystem.\n\nPeople use Hermes for software development, research, system administration, data analysis, content creation, home automation, and anything else that benefits from an AI agent with persistent context and full system access.\n\n**This skill helps you work with Hermes Agent effectively** — setting it up, configuring features, spawning additional agent instances, troubleshooting issues, finding the right commands and settings, and understanding how the system works when you need to extend or contribute to it.\n\n**Docs:** https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs/\n\n## Quick Start\n\n``​`bash\n# Install\ncurl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/main/scripts/install.sh | bash\n\n# Interactive chat (default)\nhermes\n\n# Single query\nhermes chat -q \"What is the capital of France?\"\n\n# Setup wizard\nhermes setup\n\n# Change model/provider\nhermes model\n\n# Check health\nhermes doctor\n``​`\n\n---\n\n## CLI Reference\n\n### Global Flags\n\n``​`\nhermes [flags] [command]\n\n  --version, -V             Show version\n  --resume, -r SESSION      Resume session by ID or title\n  --continue, -c [NAME]     Resume by name, or most recent session\n  --worktree, -w            Isolated git worktree mode (parallel agents)\n  --skills, -s SKILL        Preload skills (comma-separate or repeat)\n  --profile, -p NAME        Use a named profile\n  --yolo                    Skip dangerous command approval\n  --pass-session-id         Include session ID in system prompt\n``​`\n\nNo subcommand defaults to `chat`.\n\n### Chat\n\n``​`\nhermes chat [flags]\n  -q, --query TEXT          Single query, non-interactive\n  -m, --model MODEL         Model (e.g. anthropic/claude-sonnet-4)\n  -t, --toolsets LIST       Comma-separated toolsets\n  --provider PROVIDER       Force provider (openrouter, anthropic, nous, etc.)\n  -v, --verbose             Verbose output\n  -Q, --quiet               Suppress banner, spinner, tool previews\n  --checkpoints             Enable filesystem checkpoints (/rollback)\n  --source TAG              Session source tag (default: cli)\n``​`\n\n### Configuration\n\n``​`\nhermes setup [section]      Interactive wizard (model|terminal|gateway|tools|agent)\nhermes model                Interactive model/provider picker\nhermes config               View current config\nhermes config edit          Open config.yaml in $EDITOR\nhermes config set KEY VAL   Set a config value\nhermes config path          Print config.yaml path\nhermes config env-path      Print .env path\nhermes config check         Check for missing/outdated config\nhermes config migrate       Update config with new options\nhermes login [--provider P] OAuth login (nous, openai-codex)\nhermes logout               Clear stored auth\nhermes doctor [--fix]       Check dependencies and config\nhermes status [--all]       Show component status\n``​`\n\n### Tools & Skills\n\n``​`\nhermes tools                Interactive tool enable/disable (curses UI)\nhermes tools list           Show all tools and status\nhermes tools enable NAME    Enable a toolset\nhermes tools disable NAME   Disable a toolset\n\nhermes skills list          List installed skills\nhermes skills search QUERY  Search the skills hub\nhermes skills install ID    Install a skill (ID can be a hub identifier OR a direct https://…/SKILL.md URL; pass --name to override when frontmatter has no name)\nhermes skills inspect ID    Preview without installing\nhermes skills config        Enable/disable skills per platform\nhermes skills check         Check for updates\nhermes skills update        Update outdated skills\nhermes skills unins
```

```text
{"success": true, "name": "hermes-agent", "file": "references/telegram-topic-model-routing.md", "content": "# Telegram topic model routing notes\n\nUse when DJ wants different Hermes models by Telegram topic in the Chief Group.\n\n## Current Chief Group map\n\nChat: `-[REDACTED_PHONE]`\n\n- topic `1` = General/home\n- topic `3` = Archive/Old Chief\n- topic `4` = Briefings\n- topic `5` = Alerts\n- topic `6` = Daily Brain Dump\n- topic `7` = Coding\n- topic `8` = General (DJ's ad-hoc/conversational topic)\n\n## Default routing\n\n- Default (all topics unless overridden): `openai-codex` / `gpt-5.4-mini`\n- Coding topic 7: `openai-codex` / `gpt-5.5`\n- Fallback chain: `openrouter` / `deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro` → `anthropic` / `claude-opus-4-6`\n\n## Per-topic model overrides (config.yaml)\n\n``​`yaml\ntelegram_topic_model_overrides:\n  \"-[REDACTED_PHONE]\":\n    \"7\":\n      provider: openai-codex\n      model: gpt-5.5\n    \"8\":\n      provider: anthropic\n      model: claude-opus-4-6   # example: DJ requested for a session\n``​`\n\nOverrides live in `/opt/data/config.yaml` under `telegram_topic_model_overrides`.\n\n**IMPORTANT: Overrides do NOT auto-revert on `/new` or session reset.** They are config-level\nand persist until manually removed. When DJ says \"for this session only\", warn them and offer\nto revert at the end of the session (or immediately when they say \"switch back to default\").\n\nTo revert topic 8 to default gpt-5.4-mini:\n``​`yaml\n    \"8\":\n      provider: openai-codex\n      model: gpt-5.4-mini\n``​`\nOr remove the `\"8\"` block entirely.\n\nAlso revert the **global default** if it was changed during the session:\n``​`bash\n/opt/hermes/.venv/bin/hermes config set model.provider openai-codex\n/opt/hermes/.venv/bin/hermes config set model.default gpt-5.4-mini\n``​`\n\n## Session-scoped vs config-level overrides\n\n- **Session-scoped** (via `/model` slash command in-session): stored in `_session_model_overrides`\n  dict in gateway memory, cleared on `/new` or gateway restart.\n- **Config-level** (editing `config.yaml`): survives restarts and new sessions.\n\nDJ's \"for this session only\" requests map to config-level changes (we don't have a better\nmechanism). Always clean up at the end or when DJ says \"switch back to default\".\n\n## How to apply a topic override (preferred method)\n\n`hermes config set` is unreliable for deeply nested YAML keys. Patch config.yaml directly:\n\n``​`python\n# Read the section and patch:\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport re\np = Path('/opt/data/config.yaml')\n# ... use patch() tool or manual YAML edit\n``​`\n\nOr use the `patch` file tool to do a targeted text replacement on the relevant block.\nVerify after with `tail -10 /opt/data/config.yaml`.\n\n## Anthropic Opus 4.6 fast mode\n\n- Fast mode (`speed: fast`) is supported **only on `claude-opus-4-6`**.\n- Opus 4.7+ explicitly rejects the `speed` param with HTTP 400.\n- Hermes auto-injects `speed: fast` when it detects `opus-4-6` via `_supports_fast_mode()`.\n- Effect: ~2.5x faster output throughput at the same price ($5/$25 per M input/output tokens).\n- Opus 4.6 and 4.7 are **identical price** — fast mode is the key differentiator for conversational use.\n\n## Model selection guide for DJ\n\n| Use case | Provider | Model |\n|---|---|---|\n| Default conversation | openai-codex | gpt-5.4-mini |\n| Coding | openai-codex | gpt-5.5 |\n| Fast conversational Anthropic | anthropic | claude-opus-4-6 (fast mode auto) |\n| Latest Anthropic | anthropic | claude-opus-4-7 (no fast mode, same price) |\n| Compression / auxiliary | anthropic | claude-sonnet-4-6 |\n\nNote: \"Claude Opus 4.8\" does not exist in Hermes or Anthropic's lineup as of this session.\nCurrent Hermes build knows: `claude-opus-4-5`, `claude-opus-4-6`, `claude-opus-4-7`.\n\"Sonnet 4.8\" also does not exist — confirm model names against `usage_pricing.py` if unsure.\n\n## Verification after a change\n\n``​`bash\ntail -5 /opt/data/config.yaml\n/opt/hermes/.venv/bin/hermes config | grep -A4 \"Model:\"\n``​`\n\nNote: the running gateway picks up config.yaml changes on the next inbound message — no\nrestart needed for topic overrides. The current in-flight session still uses the old model\nuntil the next turn.\n\n## Communication pitfall\n\nDJ uses \"API\" to mean paid OpenAI API-key billing. Hermes logs say `API call` for any model\nrequest, including OAuth Codex. When explaining, say: \"Hermes logged a model request, but it\nis routed through `openai-codex` OAuth at `chatgpt.com/backend-api/codex`, not the paid\n`api.openai.com/v1` provider.\" Keep this concise.\n", "file_type": ".md"}
```

```text
{"code":"import sqlite3, json\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nconn=sqlite3.connect('/opt/data/spend.db')\nconn.row_factory=sqlite3.Row\nrows=conn.execute(\"\"\"\nSELECT thread_id, chat_name, channel_label, project_slug, workdir, provider, model, SUM(estimated_cost_usd) AS cost, SUM(total_tokens) AS tokens, COUNT(*) AS events\nFROM llm_usage_events\nWHERE platform='telegram' AND created_at >= datetime('now','-24 hours')\nGROUP BY thread_id, chat_name, channel_label, project_slug, workdir, provider, model\nORDER BY cost DESC\n\"\"\").fetchall()\nfor r in rows[:50]:\n    print(dict(r))\nprint('count', len(rows))\n"}
```

```text
{"code":"import re, glob, sqlite3, json, os\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# Build session_id -> thread_id map from gateway logs.\npattern_flush = re.compile(r'Flushing (?:text|photo|audio|video) batch .*telegram:group:(-?\\d+):(\\d+)')\npattern_turn = re.compile(r'conversation turn: session=([0-9_]+_[0-9a-f]+) .* platform=telegram')\ncurrent_thread = None\nsession_to_thread = {}\nfiles = sorted(glob.glob('/opt/data/logs/gateway.log*')) + sorted(glob.glob('/opt/data/logs/agent.log*'))\n# read all logs in chronological-ish file order\nfor path in files:\n    try:\n        with open(path, 'r', errors='ignore') as f:\n            for line in f:\n                m = pattern_flush.search(line)\n                if m:\n                    current_thread = m.group(2)\n                    continue\n                m = pattern_turn.search(line)\n                if m and current_thread is not None:\n                    session_to_thread[m.group(1)] = current_thread\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\nprint('mapped sessions', len(session_to_thread))\n# Sample mappings\nfor k in list(session_to_thread)[:10]:\n    print(k, session_to_thread[k])\n\n# Join with spend db telegram sessions recent 7d\nconn=sqlite3.connect('/opt/data/spend.db')\nconn.row_factory=sqlite3.Row\nrows=conn.execute(\"\"\"\nSELECT session_id, SUM(estimated_cost_usd) AS cost, SUM(total_tokens) AS tokens, COUNT(*) AS events\nFROM llm_usage_events\nWHERE platform='telegram'\n  AND created_at >= strftime('%s','now','-7 days')\nGROUP BY session_id\nORDER BY cost DESC\nLIMIT 20\n\"\"\").fetchall()\nprint('\\nTop telegram session_ids last 7d:')\nfor r in rows:\n    sid=r['session_id']\n    print(sid, 'thread', session_to_thread.get(sid), 'cost', r['cost'], 'tokens', r['tokens'], 'events', r['events'])\n"}
```

```text
{"code":"import re, glob, sqlite3, json, os\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n\n# Topic map from hermes docs/skill\nTOPIC_NAMES = {\n    '1': 'General/home',\n    '3': 'Archive/Old Chief',\n    '4': 'Briefings',\n    '5': 'Alerts',\n    '6': 'Daily Brain Dump',\n    '7': 'Coding',\n    '8': \"General (ad-hoc/conversational)\",\n}\n\npattern_flush = re.compile(r'Flushing (?:text|photo|audio|video) batch .*telegram:group:(-?\\d+):(\\d+)')\npattern_turn = re.compile(r'conversation turn: session=([0-9_]+_[0-9a-f]+) .* platform=telegram')\ncurrent_thread = None\nsession_to_thread = {}\nfor path in sorted(glob.glob('/opt/data/logs/gateway.log*')) + sorted(glob.glob('/opt/data/logs/agent.log*')):\n    try:\n        with open(path, 'r', errors='ignore') as f:\n            for line in f:\n                m = pattern_flush.search(line)\n                if m:\n                    current_thread = m.group(2)\n                    continue\n                m = pattern_turn.search(line)\n                if m and current_thread is not None:\n                    session_to_thread.setdefault(m.group(1), current_thread)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\nconn = sqlite3.connect('/opt/data/spend.db')\nconn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n\ndef aggregate(days):\n    threshold = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() - days*86400\n    rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT session_id, SUM(estimated_cost_usd) AS cost, SUM(total_tokens) AS tokens, COUNT(*) AS events\n        FROM llm_usage_events\n        WHERE platform='telegram' AND created_at >= ?\n        GROUP BY session_id\n    \"\"\", (threshold,)).fetchall()\n    by_topic = defaultdict(lambda: {'cost': 0.0, 'tokens': 0, 'events': 0, 'sessions': 0, 'unmapped_sessions': 0})\n    unmapped = []\n    for r in rows:\n        sid = r['session_id'] or ''\n        thread = session_to_thread.get(sid)\n        topic_name = TOPIC_NAMES.get(thread, f'topic {thread}' if thread else 'unmapped')\n        d = by_topic[topic_name]\n        d['cost'] += float(r['cost'] or 0.0)\n        d['tokens'] += int(r['tokens'] or 0)\n        d['events'] += int(r['events'] or 0)\n        d['sessions'] += 1\n        if thread is None:\n            d['unmapped_sessions'] += 1\n            unmapped.append((sid, r['cost'], r['tokens']))\n    return rows, by_topic, unmapped\n\nfor days in (1,7):\n    rows, by_topic, unmapped = aggregate(days)\n    print(f'\\n=== {days}d telegram topic breakdown ===')\n    total_cost = sum(v['cost'] for v in by_topic.values())\n    total_tokens = sum(v['tokens'] for v in by_topic.values())\n    print('total cost', round(total_cost, 6), 'tokens', total_tokens, 'sessions', len(rows), 'unmapped sessions', len(unmapped))\n    for topic, d in sorted(by_topic.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]['cost'], reverse=True):\n        print(f\"- {topic}: ${d['cost']:.4f} · {d['tokens']:,} tokens · {d['sessions']} sessions\" + (f\" · unmapped {d['unmapped_sessions']}\" if d['unmapped_sessions'] else ''))\n    if unmapped:\n        print('sample unmapped:', unmapped[:5])\n"}
```

```text
{"code":"import re, glob, sqlite3, json\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom agent.spend_ledger import PROVIDER_COST_CALIBRATION\n\nTOPIC_NAMES = {'1':'General/home','3':'Archive/Old Chief','4':'Briefings','5':'Alerts','6':'Daily Brain Dump','7':'Coding','8':'General (ad-hoc/conversational)'}\npattern_flush = re.compile(r'Flushing (?:text|photo|audio|video) batch .*telegram:group:(-?\\d+):(\\d+)')\npattern_turn = re.compile(r'conversation turn: session=([0-9_]+_[0-9a-f]+) .* platform=telegram')\ncurrent_thread=None\nsession_to_thread={}\nfor path in sorted(glob.glob('/opt/data/logs/gateway.log*')) + sorted(glob.glob('/opt/data/logs/agent.log*')):\n    try:\n        with open(path,'r',errors='ignore') as f:\n            for line in f:\n                m=pattern_flush.search(line)\n                if m:\n                    current_thread=m.group(2)\n                    continue\n                m=pattern_turn.search(line)\n                if m and current_thread is not None:\n                    session_to_thread.setdefault(m.group(1), current_thread)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\nconn=sqlite3.connect('/opt/data/spend.db')\nconn.row_factory=sqlite3.Row\nthreshold = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() - 7*86400\nrows=conn.execute(\"\"\"\nSELECT session_id, provider, SUM(estimated_cost_usd) AS cost, SUM(total_tokens) AS tokens, COUNT(*) AS events\nFROM llm_usage_events\nWHERE platform='telegram' AND created_at >= ?\nGROUP BY session_id, provider\n\"\"\", (threshold,)).fetchall()\nby_topic=defaultdict(lambda:{'raw':0.0,'cal':0.0,'tokens':0,'sessions':0,'providers':defaultdict(float)})\nfor r in rows:\n    sid=r['session_id'] or ''\n    thread=session_to_thread.get(sid)\n    topic=TOPIC_NAMES.get(thread, f'topic {thread}' if thread else 'unmapped')\n    provider=r['provider'] or 'unknown'\n    raw=float(r['cost'] or 0.0)\n    cal=raw*PROVIDER_COST_CALIBRATION.get(provider,1.0)\n    d=by_topic[topic]\n    d['raw']+=raw\n    d['cal']+=cal\n    d['tokens']+=int(r['tokens'] or 0)\n    d['sessions']+=1\n    d['providers'][provider]+=cal\n\nprint('7d calibrated topic breakdown:')\nfor topic,d in sorted(by_topic.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]['cal'], reverse=True):\n    print(f\"- {topic}: ${d['cal']:.2f} · raw ${d['raw']:.2f} · {d['tokens']:,} tokens · {d['sessions']} provider-sessions\")\n    prov = sorted(d['providers'].items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)\n    print('  providers:', ', '.join(f'{p} ${v:.2f}' for p,v in prov[:3]))\n"}
```

```text
{"mode":"patch","patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /opt/hermes/agent/spend_ledger.py\n@@\n-import json\n+import glob\n+import json\n import os\n+import re\n import sqlite3\n import time\n@@\n PROVIDER_COST_CALIBRATION = {\n     \"anthropic\": 0.7193,\n }\n+\n+TELEGRAM_TOPIC_NAMES = {\n+    \"1\": \"General/home\",\n+    \"3\": \"Archive/Old Chief\",\n+    \"4\": \"Briefings\",\n+    \"5\": \"Alerts\",\n+    \"6\": \"Daily Brain Dump\",\n+    \"7\": \"Coding\",\n+    \"8\": \"General (ad-hoc/conversational)\",\n+}\n+\n+_TELEGRAM_FLUSH_RE = re.compile(\n+    r\"Flushing (?:text|photo|audio|video) batch .*telegram:group:(-?\\d+):(\\d+)\"\n+)\n+_TELEGRAM_SESSION_TURN_RE = re.compile(\n+    r\"conversation turn: session=([0-9_]+_[0-9a-f]+) .* platform=telegram\"\n+)\n@@\n def _coerce_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:\n@@\n     except Exception:\n         return default\n+\n+\n+def _load_telegram_session_thread_map() -> dict[str, str]:\n+    \"\"\"Best-effort mapping from Telegram session_id to thread_id.\n+\n+    Backfilled spend rows often have platform='telegram' but empty thread/channel\n+    metadata. Gateway logs still contain the thread id next to the inbound batch,\n+    so we recover it here for report-layer labeling.\n+    \"\"\"\n+\n+    session_to_thread: dict[str, str] = {}\n+    current_thread: str | None = None\n+    log_paths = sorted(glob.glob(\"/opt/data/logs/gateway.log*\")) + sorted(glob.glob(\"/opt/data/logs/agent.log*\"))\n+    for path in log_paths:\n+        try:\n+            with open(path, \"r\", errors=\"ignore\") as handle:\n+                for line in handle:\n+                    flush_match = _TELEGRAM_FLUSH_RE.search(line)\n+                    if flush_match:\n+                        current_thread = flush_match.group(2)\n+                        continue\n+                    turn_match = _TELEGRAM_SESSION_TURN_RE.search(line)\n+                    if turn_match and current_thread is not None:\n+                        session_to_thread.setdefault(turn_match.group(1), current_thread)\n+        except Exception:\n+            continue\n+    return session_to_thread\n*** End Patch"}
```
